<details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_read_only · `toolu_01WcVKfxNWgYBRsRZnufscf2`</summary>

```json
{
  "server": "bjb1-18228",
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport subprocess, json, glob, os\nos.chdir('/root/acr-vla-conformal-refinement')\nr = subprocess.run(['ps', 'aux'], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint('=== PROCESSES ===')\nfor line in r.stdout.split('\\n'):\n    if 'evaluate.py' in line and 'grep' not in line:\n        parts = line.split()\n        pid = parts[1]\n        cpu = parts[2]\n        mem = parts[3]\n        cmd = ' '.join(parts[10:])\n        print(f'PID={pid} CPU={cpu}% MEM={mem}% CMD={cmd}')\nprint('=== LOGS ===')\nfor f in sorted(glob.glob('logs/eval_*.json')):\n    try:\n        d = json.load(open(f))\n        tasks = d.get('task_results', d.get('results', {}))\n        n = len(tasks)\n        if isinstance(tasks, dict):\n            srs = [v.get('success_rate', v.get('sr', -1)) for v in tasks.values() if isinstance(v, dict)]\n            avg_sr = sum(s for s in srs if s >= 0) / max(len([s for s in srs if s >= 0]), 1)\n            print(f'{os.path.basename(f)}: {n}/10 tasks, avg_sr={avg_sr:.1f}%')\n            for k, v in tasks.items():\n                sr = v.get('success_rate', v.get('sr', 'N/A')) if isinstance(v, dict) else v\n                print(f'  {k}: {sr}')\n        else:\n            print(f'{os.path.basename(f)}: {n}/10 tasks')\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f'{os.path.basename(f)}: error {e}')\nprint('=== GPU ===')\nr2 = subprocess.run(['nvidia-smi', '--query-gpu=index,utilization.gpu,memory.used,memory.total', '--format=csv,noheader'], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(r2.stdout)\n\"\n",
  "account": "root"
}
```

{
  "server": "bjb2-40593",
  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport subprocess, json, glob, os\nos.chdir('/root/acr-vla-conformal-refinement')\nr = subprocess.run(['ps', 'aux'], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint('=== PROCESSES ===')\nfor line in r.stdout.split('\\n'):\n    if 'evaluate.py' in line and 'grep' not in line:\n        parts = line.split()\n        pid = parts[1]\n        cpu = parts[2]\n        mem = parts[3]\n        cmd = ' '.join(parts[10:])\n        print(f'PID={pid} CPU={cpu}% MEM={mem}% CMD={cmd}')\nprint('=== LOGS ===')\nfor f in sorted(glob.glob('logs/eval_*.json')):\n    try:\n        d = json.load(open(f))\n        tasks = d.get('task_results', d.get('results', {}))\n        n = len(tasks)\n        if isinstance(tasks, dict):\n            srs = [v.get('success_rate', v.get('sr', -1)) for v in tasks.values() if isinstance(v, dict)]\n            avg_sr = sum(s for s in srs if s >= 0) / max(len([s for s in srs if s >= 0]), 1)\n            print(f'{os.path.basename(f)}: {n}/10 tasks, avg_sr={avg_sr:.1f}%')\n            for k, v in tasks.items():\n                sr = v.get('success_rate', v.get('sr', 'N/A')) if isinstance(v, dict) else v\n                print(f'  {k}: {sr}')\n        else:\n            print(f'{os.path.basename(f)}: {n}/10 tasks')\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f'{os.path.basename(f)}: error {e}')\nprint('=== GPU ===')\nr2 = subprocess.run(['nvidia-smi', '--query-gpu=index,utilization.gpu,memory.used,memory.total', '--format=csv,noheader'], capture_output=True, text=True)\nprint(r2.stdout)\n\"\n",
  "account": "root"
}
